Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the classical_mechanics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 474 and the convergence rate is O(84/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by classical_mechanics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 474, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 84. QED.
